P1148|Heading 11| پیوست الف: کد پایتون شبیه‌سازی
P1149|Body Text| کد زیر تمام معادله‌های حرکت، مدل جو، مدل پسا و رخداد عمر را پیاده‌سازی می‌کند. همه‌ی محاسبه‌های این پایان‌نامه با پایتون انجام شده‌اند؛ اجراهای فصل سوم و چهارم با همین فایل‌ها و کتابخانه‌های NumPy و SciPy تولید شده‌اند. نسخه‌ی کد در مخزن پژوهش نگه‌داری می‌شود.
P1150|Heading 31| الف-۱: full_prop.py (انتشار عددی کامل: مدل A)
P1151|Normal| """full_prop.py - Model A (numerical, full dynamics), faster implementation.
P1153|Normal| Same force model as decay_sim.accel_full (2-body, J2, co-rotating drag with
P1154|Normal| exponential atmosphere, Sun and Moon third-body, cannonball SRP with cylindrical
P1155|Normal| shadow). Differences from accel_full, all for speed and documented in the thesis:
P1156|Normal| * Sun and Moon positions are tabulated every 1800 s (linear interpolation).
P1157|Normal| Position error of the Moon from this interpolation is < 1.5 km, i.e. < 0.01 %
P1158|Normal| of the lunar tidal acceleration.
P1159|Normal| * Scalar arithmetic in the right-hand side instead of small numpy arrays.
P1160|Normal| Perigee passages are located exactly with the event function r.v = 0 (not by
P1161|Normal| sampling), and the lifetime events (r = R_E + 175 km and R_E + 120 km) are terminal.
P1162|Normal| """
P1163|Normal| import math
P1164|Normal| import time
P1165|Normal| import numpy as np
P1166|Normal| from scipy.integrate import solve_ivp
P1167|Normal| import decay_sim as ds
P1169|Normal| MU = ds.MU_E
P1170|Normal| RE = ds.R_E
P1171|Normal| J2 = ds.J2
P1172|Normal| WE = ds.W_E
P1173|Normal| MUS = ds.MU_S
P1174|Normal| MUM = ds.MU_M
P1175|Normal| AU = ds.AU
P1176|Normal| PSR = ds.P_SR
P1177|Normal| CR = ds.CR
P1178|Normal| YR_S = 365.25 * 86400.0
P1179|Normal| TAB_DT = 1800.0
P1180|Normal| GRID_OK = True
P1183|Normal| def tables(t_end, jd0=None):
P1184|Normal| if jd0 is None:
P1185|Normal| jd0 = ds.JD_EPOCH
P1186|Normal| n = int(t_end / TAB_DT) + 4
P1187|Normal| jd = jd0 + np.arange(n) * TAB_DT / 86400.0
P1188|Normal| S = ds.sun_eci(jd).T.tolist()
P1189|Normal| M = ds.moon_eci(jd).T.tolist()
P1190|Normal| return S, M
P1193|Normal| def initial_state(hp_km, argp_deg=0.0, raan_deg=0.0, inc_deg=27.0, ha_km=35786.0):
P1194|Normal| """Osculating GTO state at perigee. argp_deg = 0 reproduces decay_sim.gto_initial_state."""
P1195|Normal| rp = RE + hp_km * 1e3
P1196|Normal| ra = RE + ha_km * 1e3
P1197|Normal| a = 0.5 * (rp + ra)
P1198|Normal| e = (ra - rp) / (ra + rp)
P1199|Normal| vp = math.sqrt(MU * (1.0 + e) / rp)
P1200|Normal| r_pf = np.array([rp, 0.0, 0.0])
P1201|Normal| v_pf = np.array([0.0, vp, 0.0])
P1202|Normal| i = math.radians(inc_deg)
P1203|Normal| O = math.radians(raan_deg)
P1204|Normal| w = math.radians(argp_deg)
P1206|Normal| def Rz(t):
P1207|Normal| c, s = math.cos(t), math.sin(t)
P1208|Normal| return np.array([[c, -s, 0.0], [s, c, 0.0], [0.0, 0.0, 1.0]])
P1210|Normal| def Rx(t):
P1211|Normal| c, s = math.cos(t), math.sin(t)
P1212|Normal| return np.array([[1.0, 0.0, 0.0], [0.0, c, -s], [0.0, s, c]])
P1214|Normal| R = Rz(O) @ Rx(i) @ Rz(w)
P1215|Normal| return np.concatenate([R @ r_pf, R @ v_pf])
P1218|Normal| def rho_scalar(h_m, f107, ap, T_cache=None):
P1219|Normal| h = h_m / 1000.0
P1220|Normal| if h < 0.0 or h > 1000.0:
P1221|Normal| return 0.0
P1222|Normal| hc = min(max(h, 0.0), 1000.0)
P1223|Normal| T = 900.0 + 2.5 * (f107 - 70.0) + 1.5 * ap
P1224|Normal| H = T / (27.0 - 0.012 * (hc - 200.0))
P1225|Normal| return 6e-10 * math.exp(-(hc - 175.0) / H)
P1228|Normal| def propagate(hp0_km, argp_deg=0.0, a_sail=0.0, sail_factor=1.0, deploy_day=0.0,
P1229|Normal| t_max_yr=25.0, f107=130.0, ap=10.0, cd=ds.CD, third=True, srp=True,
P1230|Normal| rtol=1e-9, atol_r=1e-3, atol_v=1e-7, stop_km=(175.0, 120.0),
P1231|Normal| raan_deg=0.0, jd0=None,
P1232|Normal| inc_deg=27.0, ha_km=35786.0, am_nat=None, am_sail=None,
P1233|Normal| am_srp_nat=None, am_srp_sail=None,
P1234|Normal| f107_series=None, ap_series=None, sample_step_s=None, atm_model=None,
P1235|Normal| event_km=(175.0, 120.0), max_step_s=np.inf):
P1236|Normal| """Model A propagation of the osculating state.
P1238|Normal| Returns dict: peri_t_yr, peri_hp_km (every perigee passage), t_stop_yr {km: years or inf}.
P1240|Normal| The optional keyword arguments extend the model to cases other than the baseline
P1241|Normal| GTO stage. Their defaults reproduce the published Chapter 3 results exactly:
P1242|Normal| inc_deg, ha_km     : orbit geometry (baseline 27 deg, apogee 35786 km)
P1243|Normal| am_nat, am_sail    : area-to-mass overrides for DRAG (m^2/kg); default from
P1244|Normal| ds.composite_am
P1245|Normal| am_srp_nat, am_srp_sail : area-to-mass overrides for SOLAR RADIATION PRESSURE.
P1246|Normal| The baseline uses the same value as drag (cannonball sail),
P1247|Normal| which is the right approximation only when the sail and the
P1248|Normal| body present the same area to the flow. For a drag flag whose
P1249|Normal| projected area differs from the body, they must be set apart.
P1250|Normal| f107_series, ap_series : (times_s, values) arrays for a time-varying atmosphere;
P1251|Normal| default is the constant f107/ap of the baseline
P1252|Normal| sample_step_s      : if given, also return the sampled state history
P1253|Normal| atm_model          : optional nrlmsis_atm.NrlmsisAtm instance; when given, the
P1254|Normal| density comes from NRLMSIS 2.0 instead of the exponential
P1255|Normal| model of Chen et al. (2024). The default (None) is the
P1256|Normal| exponential model, i.e. the published Chapter 3 results.
P1257|Normal| event_km           : radius thresholds (km) whose first inbound crossing is
P1258|Normal| recorded. The default reproduces the published results.
P1259|Normal| max_step_s         : upper bound on the integrator step (s). The default (inf) is
P1260|Normal| the published setting. A bound of a few minutes makes every
P1261|Normal| perigee passage resolved, so that the radius event cannot be
P1262|Normal| missed (see Section 3-7 and README).
P1263|Normal| """
P1264|Normal| t_max = t_max_yr * YR_S
P1265|Normal| S, M = tables(t_max, jd0)
P1266|Normal| am_nat = ds.composite_am(0.0) if am_nat is None else am_nat
P1267|Normal| am_sail = ds.composite_am(a_sail, sail_factor) if am_sail is None else am_sail
P1268|Normal| am_srp_nat = am_nat if am_srp_nat is None else am_srp_nat
P1269|Normal| am_srp_sail = am_sail if am_srp_sail is None else am_srp_sail
P1270|Normal| td = deploy_day * 86400.0
P1271|Normal| k_drag = 0.5 * cd
P1272|Normal| j2c = 1.5 * J2 * MU * RE * RE
P1274|Normal| # Time-varying solar/geomagnetic indices (optional). The baseline uses the
P1275|Normal| # constant f107/ap, in which case no interpolation is done at all.
P1276|Normal| if f107_series is None:
P1277|Normal| f107_of_t = None
P1278|Normal| ap_of_t = None
P1279|Normal| else:
P1280|Normal| ft, fv = (np.asarray(x, dtype=float) for x in f107_series)
P1281|Normal| f107_of_t = lambda t: float(np.interp(t, ft, fv))
P1282|Normal| if ap_series is None:
P1283|Normal| ap_of_t = lambda t: float(ap)
P1284|Normal| else:
P1285|Normal| at, av = (np.asarray(x, dtype=float) for x in ap_series)
P1286|Normal| ap_of_t = lambda t: float(np.interp(t, at, av))
P1288|Normal| def rhs(t, y):
P1289|Normal| x, yy, z, vx, vy, vz = y[0], y[1], y[2], y[3], y[4], y[5]
P1290|Normal| r2 = x * x + yy * yy + z * z
P1291|Normal| rn = math.sqrt(r2)
P1292|Normal| r3 = r2 * rn
P1293|Normal| r5 = r3 * r2
P1294|Normal| ax = -MU * x / r3
P1295|Normal| ay = -MU * yy / r3
P1296|Normal| az = -MU * z / r3
P1297|Normal| kJ = j2c / r5
P1298|Normal| z2r2 = z * z / r2
P1299|Normal| ax += kJ * x * (5.0 * z2r2 - 1.0)
P1300|Normal| ay += kJ * yy * (5.0 * z2r2 - 1.0)
P1301|Normal| az += kJ * z * (5.0 * z2r2 - 3.0)
P1302|Normal| # drag, co-rotating atmosphere: v_rel = v - w x r, w x r = (-WE*y, WE*x, 0)
P1303|Normal| vrx = vx + WE * yy
P1304|Normal| vry = vy - WE * x
P1305|Normal| vr = math.sqrt(vrx * vrx + vry * vry + vz * vz)
P1306|Normal| am = am_nat if t < td else am_sail
P1307|Normal| h_m = rn - RE
P1308|Normal| if h_m >= 0.0:
P1309|Normal| h = h_m / 1000.0
P1310|Normal| if h <= 1000.0:
P1311|Normal| hc = h if h > 0.0 else 0.0
P1312|Normal| if atm_model is not None:
P1313|Normal| # Empirical NRLMSIS 2.0 field (latitude and time dependent).
P1314|Normal| lat_deg = math.degrees(math.asin(max(-1.0, min(1.0, z / rn))))
P1315|Normal| rho = atm_model.density(hc, lat_deg, t)
P1316|Normal| else:
P1317|Normal| f107_t = f107 if f107_of_t is None else f107_of_t(t)
P1318|Normal| ap_t = ap if ap_of_t is None else ap_of_t(t)
P1319|Normal| T_atm = 900.0 + 2.5 * (f107_t - 70.0) + 1.5 * ap_t
P1320|Normal| Hs = T_atm / (27.0 - 0.012 * (hc - 200.0))
P1321|Normal| rho = 6e-10 * math.exp(-(hc - 175.0) / Hs)
P1322|Normal| fac = -k_drag * am * rho * vr
P1323|Normal| ax += fac * vrx
P1324|Normal| ay += fac * vry
P1325|Normal| az += fac * vz
P1326|Normal| if third or srp:
P1327|Normal| idx = t / TAB_DT
P1328|Normal| i0 = int(idx)
P1329|Normal| f = idx - i0
P1330|Normal| s0 = S[i0]
P1331|Normal| s1 = S[i0 + 1]
P1332|Normal| sx = s0[0] + f * (s1[0] - s0[0])
P1333|Normal| sy = s0[1] + f * (s1[1] - s0[1])
P1334|Normal| sz = s0[2] + f * (s1[2] - s0[2])
P1335|Normal| dx, dy, dz = sx - x, sy - yy, sz - z
P1336|Normal| dd = math.sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz)
P1337|Normal| srn = math.sqrt(sx * sx + sy * sy + sz * sz)
P1338|Normal| if third:
P1339|Normal| c1 = MUS / (dd ** 3)
P1340|Normal| c2 = MUS / (srn ** 3)
P1341|Normal| ax += c1 * dx - c2 * sx
P1342|Normal| ay += c1 * dy - c2 * sy
P1343|Normal| az += c1 * dz - c2 * sz
P1344|Normal| m0 = M[i0]
P1345|Normal| m1 = M[i0 + 1]
P1346|Normal| mx = m0[0] + f * (m1[0] - m0[0])
P1347|Normal| my = m0[1] + f * (m1[1] - m0[1])
P1348|Normal| mz = m0[2] + f * (m1[2] - m0[2])
P1349|Normal| ex, ey, ez = mx - x, my - yy, mz - z
P1350|Normal| de = math.sqrt(ex * ex + ey * ey + ez * ez)
P1351|Normal| mrn = math.sqrt(mx * mx + my * my + mz * mz)
P1352|Normal| c3 = MUM / (de ** 3)
P1353|Normal| c4 = MUM / (mrn ** 3)
P1354|Normal| ax += c3 * ex - c4 * mx
P1355|Normal| ay += c3 * ey - c4 * my
P1356|Normal| az += c3 * ez - c4 * mz
P1357|Normal| if srp:
P1358|Normal| # cylindrical Earth shadow, Sun direction from the Sun vector
P1359|Normal| proj = x * sx + yy * sy + z * sz
P1360|Normal| if proj < 0.0:
P1361|Normal| # perpendicular distance to the Sun-Earth line
P1362|Normal| un = srn
P1363|Normal| ux, uy, uz = sx / un, sy / un, sz / un
P1364|Normal| px = x - proj / (un * un) * sx
P1365|Normal| py = yy - proj / (un * un) * sy
P1366|Normal| pz = z - proj / (un * un) * sz
P1367|Normal| perp = math.sqrt(px * px + py * py + pz * pz)
P1368|Normal| in_sh = perp < RE
P1369|Normal| else:
P1370|Normal| in_sh = False
P1371|Normal| if not in_sh:
P1372|Normal| P = PSR * (AU / dd) ** 2
P1373|Normal| am_srp = am_srp_nat if t < td else am_srp_sail
P1374|Normal| f_srp = -P * CR * am_srp / dd
P1375|Normal| ax += f_srp * dx
P1376|Normal| ay += f_srp * dy
P1377|Normal| az += f_srp * dz
P1378|Normal| return np.array([vx, vy, vz, ax, ay, az])
P1380|Normal| def ev_peri(t, y):
P1381|Normal| return y[0] * y[3] + y[1] * y[4] + y[2] * y[5]
P1382|Normal| ev_peri.direction = 1.0
P1384|Normal| evs = [ev_peri]
P1385|Normal| # Radius events. The baseline always records the inbound crossing of 175 km and
P1386|Normal| # 120 km; a caller may add its own thresholds (e.g. 400 km for the ALE-1 case).
P1387|Normal| for km in event_km:
P1388|Normal| rs_ = RE + km * 1e3
P1390|Normal| def ev(t, y, rs_=rs_):
P1391|Normal| return math.sqrt(y[0] * y[0] + y[1] * y[1] + y[2] * y[2]) - rs_
P1392|Normal| ev.terminal = km in stop_km  # integration stops at the first crossing of these radii
P1393|Normal| ev.direction = -1.0
P1394|Normal| evs.append(ev)
P1396|Normal| y0 = initial_state(hp0_km, argp_deg, raan_deg, inc_deg=inc_deg, ha_km=ha_km)
P1397|Normal| t_eval = None if sample_step_s is None else np.arange(0.0, t_max + 1e-6, sample_step_s)
P1398|Normal| sol = solve_ivp(rhs, (0.0, t_max), y0, method="DOP853", rtol=rtol, max_step=max_step_s,
P1399|Normal| atol=np.array([atol_r] * 3 + [atol_v] * 3), events=evs, t_eval=t_eval)
P1400|Normal| pt = sol.t_events[0]
P1401|Normal| pr = np.linalg.norm(sol.y_events[0][:, :3], axis=1) if len(pt) else np.array([])
P1402|Normal| keep = pt > 1.0
P1403|Normal| peri_t_yr = pt[keep] / YR_S
P1404|Normal| peri_hp = (pr[keep] - RE) / 1e3
P1405|Normal| # Lifetime to a radius threshold: the first perigee passage below the threshold
P1406|Normal| # (the definition of Section 3-6). The radius event is a fallback: it can miss a
P1407|Normal| # shallow dip below the threshold near perigee, which lasts only seconds, and then
P1408|Normal| # fires one or more orbits late (see README, note on the event test).
P1409|Normal| t_stop = {}
P1410|Normal| for k, km in enumerate(event_km):
P1411|Normal| tk = sol.t_events[k + 1]
P1412|Normal| t_radius = float(tk[0] / YR_S) if len(tk) else float("inf")
P1413|Normal| below = np.nonzero(peri_hp < km)[0]
P1414|Normal| t_perigee = float(peri_t_yr[below[0]]) if len(below) else float("inf")
P1415|Normal| t_stop[km] = min(t_perigee, t_radius)
P1416|Normal| out = dict(peri_t_yr=peri_t_yr, peri_hp_km=peri_hp, t_stop_yr=t_stop, status=sol.status,
P1417|Normal| t_end_yr=float(sol.t[-1] / YR_S))
P1418|Normal| if sample_step_s is not None:
P1419|Normal| rn = np.linalg.norm(sol.y[:3, :], axis=0)
P1420|Normal| out["t_s"] = sol.t
P1421|Normal| out["r_km"] = rn / 1e3
P1422|Normal| out["a_km"] = np.array([ds.elements_from_state(sol.y[:3, k], sol.y[3:, k])[0] / 1e3
P1423|Normal| for k in range(sol.t.size)])
P1424|Normal| out["e"] = np.array([ds.elements_from_state(sol.y[:3, k], sol.y[3:, k])[1]
P1425|Normal| for k in range(sol.t.size)])
P1426|Normal| out["hp_km"] = (out["a_km"] * (1.0 - out["e"]) - RE / 1e3)
P1427|Normal| return out
P1430|Normal| if __name__ == "__main__":
P1431|Normal| t0 = time.time()
P1432|Normal| out = propagate(250.0, 0.0, t_max_yr=1.0)
P1433|Normal| hp = out["peri_hp_km"]
P1434|Normal| print("ω=0 natural, 1 yr: passages", hp.size, "first", np.round(hp[:4], 3), "min %.2f max %.2f last %.2f" % (hp.min(), hp.max(), hp[-1]), "stops", out["t_stop_yr"], round(time.time() - t0, 1), "s")
P1437|Heading 31| الف-۲: decay_sim.py (ثابت‌ها، مدل B، تعریف عمر)
P1438|Normal| # -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
P1439|Normal| """
P1440|Normal| decay_sim.py
P1441|Normal| Passive disposal of a GTO upper stage with a CubeSat-deployed drag sail.
P1443|Normal| Two propagators (Python is the reference implementation):
P1445|Normal| Model A  Numerical propagation of the Cartesian state (Earth-centred inertial).
P1446|Normal| Accelerations: 2-body + J2 + atmospheric drag (atmosphere co-rotates
P1447|Normal| with the Earth) + Sun and Moon third-body + cannonball solar radiation
P1448|Normal| pressure with a cylindrical Earth shadow.
P1450|Normal| Model B  Orbit-averaged drag model. Gauss-type rates for the semi-major axis a
P1451|Normal| and the semi-latus rectum p (hence e) are averaged over one orbit using
P1452|Normal| the eccentric anomaly E, including atmospheric co-rotation. Integrated
P1453|Normal| with RK4 and an adaptive step. Used for lifetime studies over decades.
P1455|Normal| Atmosphere (both models), exponential form of Chen et al. (2024), Aerospace
P1456|Normal| 11(4):315, Eqs. (3)-(5):
P1457|Normal| rho(h) = 6e-10 * exp(-(h - 175) / H),   H = T / m,
P1458|Normal| T = 900 + 2.5 (F10.7 - 70) + 1.5 Ap,    m = 27 - 0.012 (h - 200),
P1459|Normal| with h in km and rho in kg/m^3. Applied for 0 <= h <= 1000 km.
P1461|Normal| Units: SI (m, s, kg) internally. Altitudes in reports are in km.
P1462|Normal| """
P1463|Normal| import numpy as np
P1464|Normal| from scipy.integrate import solve_ivp
P1466|Normal| # ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
P1467|Normal| # Constants (SI)
P1468|Normal| # ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
P1469|Normal| MU_E = 3.986004418e14       # m^3 s^-2, Earth gravitational parameter
P1470|Normal| R_E = 6378137.0             # m, Earth equatorial radius (WGS-84)
P1471|Normal| J2 = 1.08262668e-3          # Earth oblateness coefficient
P1472|Normal| W_E = 7.2921159e-5          # rad s^-1, Earth rotation rate
P1473|Normal| MU_S = 1.32712440018e20     # m^3 s^-2, Sun
P1474|Normal| MU_M = 4.9028695e12         # m^3 s^-2, Moon
P1475|Normal| AU = 1.495978707e11         # m
P1476|Normal| P_SR = 4.56e-6              # N m^-2, solar radiation pressure at 1 AU
P1477|Normal| JD_J2000 = 2451545.0
P1478|Normal| JD_EPOCH = 2461041.5        # 2026-01-01 00:00 UT (Julian date)
P1479|Normal| OBLIQ_DEG = 23.439          # mean obliquity of the ecliptic (deg)
P1481|Normal| # ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
P1482|Normal| # Mission and spacecraft parameters (baseline; see thesis Chapter 3)
P1483|Normal| # ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
P1484|Normal| HA_KM = 35786.0             # GTO apogee altitude (km)
P1485|Normal| INC_DEG = 27.0              # GTO inclination (deg)
P1486|Normal| COS_I = np.cos(np.radians(INC_DEG))
P1487|Normal| M_STAGE = 2000.0            # kg, separated upper stage
P1488|Normal| D_STAGE = 2.6               # m, stage diameter
P1489|Normal| L_STAGE = 5.0               # m, stage length
P1490|Normal| # Tumbling-averaged cross-section of a convex body = total surface / 4 (Cauchy)
P1491|Normal| A_STAGE = (np.pi * D_STAGE * L_STAGE + np.pi * D_STAGE ** 2 / 2.0) / 4.0   # m^2
P1492|Normal| M_MOD = 12.0                # kg, 6U-12U CubeSat-class module
P1493|Normal| SIGMA_SAIL = 0.1            # kg m^-2, areal density of the membrane sail (assumed)
P1494|Normal| CD = 2.2                    # drag coefficient (assumed)
P1495|Normal| CR = 1.3                    # SRP coefficient (assumed)
P1498|Normal| def composite_am(a_sail, sail_factor=1.0):
P1499|Normal| """Area-to-mass ratio (m^2/kg) of stage + module + sail.
P1501|Normal| sail_factor = 1.0 : sail presents its full area to the flow (ideal attitude)
P1502|Normal| sail_factor = 0.5 : sail presents half its area on average (tumbling)
P1503|Normal| """
P1504|Normal| area = A_STAGE + sail_factor * a_sail
P1505|Normal| mass = M_STAGE + M_MOD + SIGMA_SAIL * a_sail
P1506|Normal| return area / mass
P1509|Normal| # ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
P1510|Normal| # Atmosphere
P1511|Normal| # ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
P1512|Normal| def rho_atm(h_m, f107=130.0, ap=10.0):
P1513|Normal| """Exponential density (kg/m^3), Chen et al. (2024) Eqs. (3)-(5). h_m in metres."""
P1514|Normal| h = np.asarray(h_m, dtype=float) / 1000.0                       # km
P1515|Normal| h_c = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1000.0)
P1516|Normal| T = 900.0 + 2.5 * (f107 - 70.0) + 1.5 * ap                      # K
P1517|Normal| H = T / (27.0 - 0.012 * (h_c - 200.0))                          # km
P1518|Normal| rho = 6e-10 * np.exp(-(h_c - 175.0) / H)
P1519|Normal| return np.where((h < 0.0) | (h > 1000.0), 0.0, rho)
P1522|Normal| # ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
P1523|Normal| # Low-precision ephemerides (Sun, Moon) in the equatorial inertial frame
P1524|Normal| # ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
P1525|Normal| def sun_eci(jd):
P1526|Normal| """Geocentric Sun position (m), low-precision series."""
P1527|Normal| n = jd - JD_J2000
P1528|Normal| L = np.radians((280.460 + 0.9856474 * n) % 360.0)
P1529|Normal| g = np.radians((357.528 + 0.9856003 * n) % 360.0)
P1530|Normal| lam = L + np.radians(1.915 * np.sin(g) + 0.020 * np.sin(2.0 * g))
P1531|Normal| R = 1.00014 - 0.01671 * np.cos(g) - 0.00014 * np.cos(2.0 * g)   # AU
P1532|Normal| eps = np.radians(OBLIQ_DEG - 0.0000004 * n)
P1533|Normal| return R * AU * np.array([np.cos(lam),
P1534|Normal| np.sin(lam) * np.cos(eps),
P1535|Normal| np.sin(lam) * np.sin(eps)])
P1538|Normal| def moon_eci(jd):
P1539|Normal| """Geocentric Moon position (m), truncated lunar series (Meeus-type)."""
P1540|Normal| T = (jd - JD_J2000) / 36525.0
P1541|Normal| d = np.radians
P1542|Normal| lam = (218.32 + 481267.8813 * T
P1543|Normal| + 6.29 * np.sin(d(134.9 + 477198.85 * T))
P1544|Normal| - 1.27 * np.sin(d(259.2 - 413335.38 * T))
P1545|Normal| + 0.66 * np.sin(d(235.7 + 890534.23 * T))
P1546|Normal| + 0.21 * np.sin(d(269.9 + 954397.70 * T))
P1547|Normal| - 0.19 * np.sin(d(357.5 + 35999.05 * T))
P1548|Normal| - 0.11 * np.sin(d(186.5 + 966404.05 * T)))
P1549|Normal| beta = (5.13 * np.sin(d(93.3 + 483202.03 * T))
P1550|Normal| + 0.28 * np.sin(d(228.2 + 960400.87 * T))
P1551|Normal| - 0.28 * np.sin(d(318.3 + 6003.18 * T))
P1552|Normal| - 0.17 * np.sin(d(217.6 - 407332.20 * T)))
P1553|Normal| par = (0.9508 + 0.0518 * np.cos(d(134.9 + 477198.85 * T))
P1554|Normal| + 0.0095 * np.cos(d(259.2 - 413335.38 * T))
P1555|Normal| + 0.0078 * np.cos(d(235.7 + 890534.23 * T))
P1556|Normal| + 0.0028 * np.cos(d(269.9 + 954397.70 * T)))
P1557|Normal| dist = R_E / np.sin(d(par))
P1558|Normal| lr, br = d(lam % 360.0), d(beta)
P1559|Normal| x_e = dist * np.cos(br) * np.cos(lr)
P1560|Normal| y_e = dist * np.cos(br) * np.sin(lr)
P1561|Normal| z_e = dist * np.sin(br)
P1562|Normal| eps = d(OBLIQ_DEG - 0.0000004 * (jd - JD_J2000))
P1563|Normal| return np.array([x_e,
P1564|Normal| y_e * np.cos(eps) - z_e * np.sin(eps),
P1565|Normal| y_e * np.sin(eps) + z_e * np.cos(eps)])
P1568|Normal| # ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
P1569|Normal| # Initial conditions
P1570|Normal| # ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
P1571|Normal| def gto_initial_state(hp_km, ha_km=HA_KM, inc_deg=INC_DEG):
P1572|Normal| """Osculating state at perigee: perigee on +x axis, omega = Omega = 0."""
P1573|Normal| rp = R_E + hp_km * 1e3
P1574|Normal| ra = R_E + ha_km * 1e3
P1575|Normal| a0 = 0.5 * (rp + ra)
P1576|Normal| e0 = (ra - rp) / (ra + rp)
P1577|Normal| vp = np.sqrt(MU_E * (2.0 / rp - 1.0 / a0))
P1578|Normal| i = np.radians(inc_deg)
P1579|Normal| r0 = np.array([rp, 0.0, 0.0])
P1580|Normal| v0 = np.array([0.0, vp * np.cos(i), vp * np.sin(i)])
P1581|Normal| return a0, e0, np.concatenate([r0, v0])
P1584|Normal| def elements_from_state(r, v):
P1585|Normal| """Osculating semi-major axis (m) and eccentricity from a Cartesian state."""
P1586|Normal| rn = np.linalg.norm(r)
P1587|Normal| energy = 0.5 * (v @ v) - MU_E / rn
P1588|Normal| a = -MU_E / (2.0 * energy)
P1589|Normal| h = np.cross(r, v)
P1590|Normal| e_vec = np.cross(v, h) / MU_E - r / rn
P1591|Normal| return a, np.linalg.norm(e_vec)
P1594|Normal| def _j2_only_acc(r):
P1595|Normal| rn = np.linalg.norm(r)
P1596|Normal| k = 1.5 * J2 * MU_E * R_E ** 2 / rn ** 5
P1597|Normal| z2r2 = r[2] * r[2] / (rn * rn)
P1598|Normal| return -MU_E * r / rn ** 3 + k * np.array([r[0] * (5.0 * z2r2 - 1.0),
P1599|Normal| r[1] * (5.0 * z2r2 - 1.0),
P1600|Normal| r[2] * (5.0 * z2r2 - 3.0)])
P1603|Normal| def mean_gto_elements(hp_km, ha_km=HA_KM, inc_deg=INC_DEG, n_samp=2000):
P1604|Normal| """Orbit-averaged (a, e) of the initial osculating state under 2-body + J2.
P1606|Normal| Short-period J2 terms are large at a 250 km perigee (tens of km in a), so the
P1607|Normal| mean elements, not the osculating ones, initialise the averaged model."""
P1608|Normal| a0, e0, y0 = gto_initial_state(hp_km, ha_km, inc_deg)
P1609|Normal| T = 2.0 * np.pi * np.sqrt(a0 ** 3 / MU_E)
P1610|Normal| sol = solve_ivp(lambda t, y: np.concatenate([y[3:], _j2_only_acc(y[:3])]),
P1611|Normal| (0.0, T), y0, method="DOP853", rtol=1e-12, atol=1e-6,
P1612|Normal| t_eval=np.linspace(0.0, T, n_samp, endpoint=False))
P1613|Normal| a_arr = np.empty(sol.t.size)
P1614|Normal| e_arr = np.empty(sol.t.size)
P1615|Normal| for k in range(sol.t.size):
P1616|Normal| a_arr[k], e_arr[k] = elements_from_state(sol.y[:3, k], sol.y[3:, k])
P1617|Normal| return a_arr.mean(), e_arr.mean()
P1620|Normal| # ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
P1621|Normal| # Model A: full numerical propagation
P1622|Normal| # ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
P1623|Normal| def accel_full(t, y, p):
P1624|Normal| """Acceleration vector for state y = [x, y, z, vx, vy, vz] (SI). t in seconds from epoch."""
P1625|Normal| r = y[:3]
P1626|Normal| v = y[3:]
P1627|Normal| rn = np.sqrt(r @ r)
P1628|Normal| acc = -MU_E * r / rn ** 3
P1630|Normal| # J2 zonal harmonic
P1631|Normal| k = 1.5 * J2 * MU_E * R_E ** 2 / rn ** 5
P1632|Normal| z2r2 = r[2] * r[2] / (rn * rn)
P1633|Normal| acc = acc + k * np.array([r[0] * (5.0 * z2r2 - 1.0),
P1634|Normal| r[1] * (5.0 * z2r2 - 1.0),
P1635|Normal| r[2] * (5.0 * z2r2 - 3.0)])
P1637|Normal| # Atmospheric drag with a co-rotating atmosphere
P1638|Normal| vrel = v - np.cross(np.array([0.0, 0.0, W_E]), r)
P1639|Normal| vr = np.sqrt(vrel @ vrel)
P1640|Normal| am = p["am"](t)
P1641|Normal| rho = float(rho_atm(rn - R_E, p["f107"], p["ap"]))
P1642|Normal| acc = acc - 0.5 * p["cd"] * am * rho * vr * vrel
P1644|Normal| jd = JD_EPOCH + t / 86400.0
P1645|Normal| if p["third_body"] or p["srp"]:
P1646|Normal| rs = sun_eci(jd)
P1647|Normal| if p["third_body"]:
P1648|Normal| rm = moon_eci(jd)
P1649|Normal| ds_ = rs - r
P1650|Normal| dm_ = rm - r
P1651|Normal| acc = acc + MU_S * (ds_ / np.linalg.norm(ds_) ** 3 - rs / np.linalg.norm(rs) ** 3)
P1652|Normal| acc = acc + MU_M * (dm_ / np.linalg.norm(dm_) ** 3 - rm / np.linalg.norm(rm) ** 3)
P1654|Normal| if p["srp"]:
P1655|Normal| u = rs / np.linalg.norm(rs)
P1656|Normal| proj = r @ u
P1657|Normal| in_shadow = (proj < 0.0) and (np.linalg.norm(r - proj * u) < R_E)
P1658|Normal| if not in_shadow:
P1659|Normal| ds_ = rs - r
P1660|Normal| dd = np.linalg.norm(ds_)
P1661|Normal| P = P_SR * (AU / dd) ** 2
P1662|Normal| acc = acc - P * CR * am * ds_ / dd
P1664|Normal| return np.concatenate([v, acc])
P1667|Normal| def run_full(hp_km, am_fn, days, f107=130.0, ap=10.0, cd=CD, third_body=True, srp=True,
P1668|Normal| out_step_s=600.0, rtol=1e-9):
P1669|Normal| """Model A. Returns dict with t_days, osculating hp_km, a_km, e."""
P1670|Normal| _, _, y0 = gto_initial_state(hp_km)
P1671|Normal| p = dict(am=am_fn, f107=f107, ap=ap, cd=cd, third_body=third_body, srp=srp)
P1672|Normal| t_end = days * 86400.0
P1673|Normal| t_eval = np.arange(0.0, t_end + 1e-6, out_step_s)
P1674|Normal| atol = np.array([1e-3, 1e-3, 1e-3, 1e-7, 1e-7, 1e-7])
P1675|Normal| sol = solve_ivp(lambda t, y: accel_full(t, y, p), (0.0, t_end), y0,
P1676|Normal| method="DOP853", rtol=rtol, atol=atol, t_eval=t_eval)
P1677|Normal| a_arr = np.empty(sol.t.size)
P1678|Normal| e_arr = np.empty(sol.t.size)
P1679|Normal| for k in range(sol.t.size):
P1680|Normal| a_arr[k], e_arr[k] = elements_from_state(sol.y[:3, k], sol.y[3:, k])
P1681|Normal| hp = (a_arr * (1.0 - e_arr) - R_E) / 1e3
P1682|Normal| return dict(t_days=sol.t / 86400.0, hp_km=hp, a_km=a_arr / 1e3, e=e_arr,
P1683|Normal| status=sol.status, message=sol.message)
P1686|Normal| # ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
P1687|Normal| # Model B: orbit-averaged drag with co-rotation
P1688|Normal| # ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
P1689|Normal| N_QUAD = 4096
P1690|Normal| _THETA = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi, N_QUAD, endpoint=False)   # eccentric anomaly grid
P1693|Normal| def averaged_rates(a, e2, am, f107=130.0, ap=10.0, cd=CD, n_quad=None):
P1694|Normal| """Orbit-averaged da/dt (m/s) and d(e^2)/dt (1/s) for atmospheric drag.
P1696|Normal| With a_D = -D v_rel, D = 0.5 Cd (A/m) rho |v_rel|, and v_rel = v - w x r:
P1697|Normal| v . v_rel = v^2 - w h cos i                 (energy rate)
P1698|Normal| h_vec-rate: dh/dt = -D (h - w r^2 cos i)    (angular momentum)
P1699|Normal| da/dt   = -(2 a^2 / mu) D (v^2 - w h cos i)
P1700|Normal| dp/dt   = -2 D p (1 - w r^2 cos i / h),     p = a (1 - e^2)
P1701|Normal| d(e^2)/dt = -(dp/dt)/a + p (da/dt)/a^2
P1702|Normal| |v_rel|^2 = v^2 - 2 w h cos i + w^2 r^2 (1 - sin^2 i / 2)
P1703|Normal| Orbit average: <X> = (1/2 pi) * integral_0^{2 pi} X (1 - e cos E) dE.
P1704|Normal| """
P1705|Normal| if n_quad is None:
P1706|Normal| theta = _THETA
P1707|Normal| else:
P1708|Normal| theta = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi, n_quad, endpoint=False)
P1709|Normal| cose = np.cos(theta)
P1710|Normal| e = np.sqrt(max(e2, 0.0))
P1711|Normal| p = a * (1.0 - e2)
P1712|Normal| w = 1.0 - e * cose
P1713|Normal| r = a * w
P1714|Normal| v2 = MU_E * (2.0 / r - 1.0 / a)
P1715|Normal| h = np.sqrt(MU_E * p)
P1716|Normal| vrel2 = v2 - 2.0 * W_E * h * COS_I + (W_E ** 2) * r * r * (1.0 - 0.5 * (1.0 - COS_I ** 2))
P1717|Normal| rho = rho_atm(r - R_E, f107, ap)
P1718|Normal| D = 0.5 * cd * am * rho * np.sqrt(vrel2)
P1719|Normal| adot = -np.mean((2.0 * a * a / MU_E) * D * (v2 - W_E * h * COS_I) * w)
P1720|Normal| pdot = -np.mean(2.0 * D * p * (1.0 - W_E * r * r * COS_I / h) * w)
P1721|Normal| e2dot = -pdot / a + p * adot / (a * a)
P1722|Normal| return adot, e2dot
P1725|Normal| def propagate_averaged(a0, e0, am_fn, stops_km=(175.0, 120.0), f107=130.0, ap=10.0, cd=CD,
P1726|Normal| t_max_days=400.0 * 365.25, dt_max_days=2.0, dh_km=0.5, n_quad=None,
P1727|Normal| record=False, record_every_days=1.0):
P1728|Normal| """Integrate Model B (RK4, adaptive step). Detects mean-perigee crossings of stops_km.
P1730|Normal| Returns dict with t_cross_days (dict, np.inf if not reached) and, if record, history.
P1731|Normal| """
P1732|Normal| a = a0
P1733|Normal| e2 = e0 * e0
P1734|Normal| t = 0.0
P1735|Normal| t_max = t_max_days * 86400.0
P1736|Normal| dt_cap = dt_max_days * 86400.0
P1737|Normal| crossings = {s: np.inf for s in stops_km}
P1738|Normal| pending = sorted(stops_km, reverse=True)
P1739|Normal| hist_t, hist_hp, hist_a, hist_e = [], [], [], []
P1740|Normal| next_rec = 0.0
P1742|Normal| def hp_of(a_, e2_):
P1743|Normal| return (a_ * (1.0 - np.sqrt(max(e2_, 0.0))) - R_E) / 1e3
P1745|Normal| while t < t_max and pending:
P1746|Normal| hp_old = hp_of(a, e2)
P1747|Normal| if record and t >= next_rec:
P1748|Normal| hist_t.append(t / 86400.0)
P1749|Normal| hist_hp.append(hp_old)
P1750|Normal| hist_a.append(a / 1e3)
P1751|Normal| hist_e.append(np.sqrt(max(e2, 0.0)))
P1752|Normal| next_rec = t + record_every_days * 86400.0
P1753|Normal| k1a, k1e = averaged_rates(a, e2, am_fn(t), f107, ap, cd, n_quad)
P1754|Normal| e = np.sqrt(max(e2, 1e-12))
P1755|Normal| hpdot = abs(k1a * (1.0 - e) - a * k1e / (2.0 * e)) / 1e3        # km/s
P1756|Normal| dt = min(dt_cap, dh_km / max(hpdot, 1e-15))
P1757|Normal| h2 = 0.5 * dt
P1758|Normal| k2a, k2e = averaged_rates(a + h2 * k1a, e2 + h2 * k1e, am_fn(t + h2), f107, ap, cd, n_quad)
P1759|Normal| k3a, k3e = averaged_rates(a + h2 * k2a, e2 + h2 * k2e, am_fn(t + h2), f107, ap, cd, n_quad)
P1760|Normal| k4a, k4e = averaged_rates(a + dt * k3a, e2 + dt * k3e, am_fn(t + dt), f107, ap, cd, n_quad)
P1761|Normal| a_new = a + dt / 6.0 * (k1a + 2.0 * k2a + 2.0 * k3a + k4a)
P1762|Normal| e2_new = e2 + dt / 6.0 * (k1e + 2.0 * k2e + 2.0 * k3e + k4e)
P1763|Normal| hp_new = hp_of(a_new, e2_new)
P1764|Normal| while pending and hp_new <= pending[0]:
P1765|Normal| s = pending.pop(0)
P1766|Normal| frac = (hp_old - s) / (hp_old - hp_new)
P1767|Normal| crossings[s] = (t + frac * dt) / 86400.0
P1768|Normal| a, e2, t = a_new, e2_new, t + dt
P1769|Normal| if e2 <= 0.0:
P1770|Normal| break
P1771|Normal| out = dict(t_cross_days=crossings)
P1772|Normal| if record:
P1773|Normal| out.update(t_days=np.array(hist_t), hp_km=np.array(hist_hp),
P1774|Normal| a_km=np.array(hist_a), e=np.array(hist_e))
P1775|Normal| return out
P1778|Normal| def lifetime_days(hp0_km, a_sail=0.0, sail_factor=1.0, f107=130.0, ap=10.0, cd=CD,
P1779|Normal| deploy_day=0.0, stops_km=(175.0, 120.0), n_quad=2048, dt_max_days=2.0,
P1780|Normal| mean0=None, t_max_days=400.0 * 365.25):
P1781|Normal| """Days from separation to mean-perigee crossing of each stop altitude (Model B).
P1783|Normal| mean0 : optional (a0, e0) mean elements; if None they are computed from the
P1784|Normal| initial osculating state (cached)."""
P1785|Normal| if mean0 is None:
P1786|Normal| a0, e0, _ = gto_initial_state(hp0_km)     # osculating state at perigee (injection)
P1787|Normal| else:
P1788|Normal| a0, e0 = mean0
P1789|Normal| am_nat = composite_am(0.0)
P1790|Normal| am_sail = composite_am(a_sail, sail_factor)
P1791|Normal| td = deploy_day * 86400.0
P1793|Normal| def am_fn(t):
P1794|Normal| return am_nat if t < td else am_sail
P1796|Normal| res = propagate_averaged(a0, e0, am_fn, stops_km=stops_km, f107=f107, ap=ap, cd=cd,
P1797|Normal| t_max_days=t_max_days, n_quad=n_quad, dt_max_days=dt_max_days)
P1798|Normal| return res["t_cross_days"]
P1801|Normal| _MEAN_CACHE = {}
P1804|Normal| def _mean0_cache(hp_km):
P1805|Normal| key = round(float(hp_km), 6)
P1806|Normal| if key not in _MEAN_CACHE:
P1807|Normal| _MEAN_CACHE[key] = mean_gto_elements(hp_km)
P1808|Normal| return _MEAN_CACHE[key]
P1811|Normal| def required_sail_area(hp0_km, target_days, sail_factor=1.0, f107=130.0, ap=10.0, cd=CD,
P1812|Normal| stop_km=175.0, a_hi=400.0, deploy_day=0.0):
P1813|Normal| """Smallest sail area (m^2) that brings the mean perigee to stop_km within target_days.
P1814|Normal| Bisection on A_sail. Returns 0.0 if no sail is needed, np.nan if a_hi is insufficient."""
P1815|Normal| horizon = 1.2 * target_days
P1817|Normal| def ok(a_sail):
P1818|Normal| t = lifetime_days(hp0_km, a_sail, sail_factor, f107, ap, cd, deploy_day, (stop_km,),
P1819|Normal| t_max_days=horizon)[stop_km]
P1820|Normal| return t <= target_days
P1821|Normal| if ok(0.0):
P1822|Normal| return 0.0
P1823|Normal| if not ok(a_hi):
P1824|Normal| return np.nan
P1825|Normal| lo, hi = 0.0, a_hi
P1826|Normal| for _ in range(60):
P1827|Normal| mid = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
P1828|Normal| if ok(mid):
P1829|Normal| hi = mid
P1830|Normal| else:
P1831|Normal| lo = mid
P1832|Normal| if hi - lo < 0.01:
P1833|Normal| break
P1834|Normal| return hi
P1837|Normal| if __name__ == "__main__":
P1838|Normal| print("A_STAGE [m^2] =", round(A_STAGE, 3), " natural A/m =", round(composite_am(0.0), 6))
P1841|Heading 31| الف-۳: nrlmsis_atm.py (جدول چگالی NRLMSIS 2.0)
P1842|Normal| # -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
P1843|Normal| """nrlmsis_atm.py - NRLMSIS 2.0 atmosphere for the Chapter 3 propagator.
P1845|Normal| The baseline model of decay_sim.rho_atm is the exponential form of Chen et al. (2024).
P1846|Normal| Test T7 (density_compare.py) showed that this model overestimates the density of
P1847|Normal| NRLMSIS 2.0 by a factor of about 1.2 to 2.3 in the 200-300 km band (local-time mean,
P1848|Normal| F10.7 = 70 to 200). Above about 450 km the sign reverses for F10.7 = 130: the
P1849|Normal| exponential density is then lower than the NRLMSIS local-time mean (factor 0.8 to 1.2
P1850|Normal| in 450-550 km), because its scale height is too large there. The comparison is therefore
P1851|Normal| height-dependent and is reported in Section 3-9-3 of the thesis (density_compare.py).
P1853|Normal| This module provides the empirical density field as a time-dependent table:
P1854|Normal| rho(altitude, latitude, t)
P1855|Normal| built from pymsis (NRLMSIS 2.0) on an (altitude, latitude) grid. Two choices make the
P1856|Normal| table a diurnal mean suitable for a multi-year lifetime:
P1857|Normal| - the density is averaged over local time (24 longitudes at one UT instant), because
P1858|Normal| a satellite samples all local times over its life and the diurnal bulge is large;
P1859|Normal| - the table is rebuilt every `rebuild_days` of simulated time. Either F10.7/Ap are
P1860|Normal| held constant (f107, ap arguments; the same indices as the exponential model, so the
P1861|Normal| two atmospheres can be compared like for like) or the historical indices of the
P1862|Normal| simulated date are used (needs the celestrak.org data file, downloaded by pymsis).
P1864|Normal| The field is pre-computed on the (altitude, latitude) grid and interpolated bilinearly
P1865|Normal| inside the right-hand side of the propagator. No call to pymsis is made during integration.
P1867|Normal| Sign convention: the returned density is in kg/m^3 and the altitude is geodetic, in km.
P1868|Normal| """
P1869|Normal| import numpy as np
P1870|Normal| import pymsis
P1871|Normal| from pymsis.utils import get_f107_ap
P1873|Normal| # Altitude grid (km). The drag is negligible below 100 km and above 600 km for the
P1874|Normal| # cases of interest.
P1875|Normal| ALT_MIN, ALT_MAX, ALT_STEP = 100.0, 600.0, 2.0
P1876|Normal| # Latitude grid (deg).
P1877|Normal| LAT_MIN, LAT_MAX, LAT_STEP = -90.0, 90.0, 15.0
P1878|Normal| # Longitudes used for the local-time average (one per 15 degrees = one per hour).
P1879|Normal| N_LON = 24
P1881|Normal| _ALTS = np.arange(ALT_MIN, ALT_MAX + 0.5 * ALT_STEP, ALT_STEP)
P1882|Normal| _LATS = np.arange(LAT_MIN, LAT_MAX + 0.5 * LAT_STEP, LAT_STEP)
P1883|Normal| _LONS = np.arange(N_LON) * 360.0 / N_LON
P1886|Normal| def _jd_to_datetime64(jd):
P1887|Normal| """Julian date(s) to numpy datetime64 in seconds (UTC)."""
P1888|Normal| unix_s = (np.atleast_1d(np.asarray(jd, dtype=float)) - 2440587.5) * 86400.0
P1889|Normal| return np.round(unix_s).astype("int64").astype("datetime64[s]")
P1892|Normal| def _indices(times_s, jd0):
P1893|Normal| """Historical F10.7, F10.7a and Ap at simulated times (s) after the epoch jd0."""
P1894|Normal| dates = _jd_to_datetime64(jd0 + np.asarray(times_s, dtype=float) / 86400.0)
P1895|Normal| f107s, f107as, aps = get_f107_ap(dates)
P1896|Normal| return (np.atleast_1d(np.asarray(f107s, dtype=float)),
P1897|Normal| np.atleast_1d(np.asarray(f107as, dtype=float)),
P1898|Normal| np.atleast_1d(np.asarray(aps, dtype=float)))
P1901|Normal| class NrlmsisAtm:
P1902|Normal| """Time-dependent NRLMSIS 2.0 density field, tabulated on (altitude, latitude).
P1904|Normal| Parameters
P1905|Normal| ----------
P1906|Normal| jd0          : Julian date of the simulation epoch (ds.JD_EPOCH by default)
P1907|Normal| rebuild_days : interval of simulated time between table rebuilds
P1908|Normal| f107, ap     : constant solar flux and Ap index; if both are None the historical
P1909|Normal| indices of each simulated date are used
P1910|Normal| alt_grid, lat_grid : optional override of the tabulation grid
P1911|Normal| """
P1913|Normal| def __init__(self, jd0, rebuild_days=30.0, f107=None, ap=None, alt_grid=None, lat_grid=None):
P1914|Normal| if (f107 is None) != (ap is None):
P1915|Normal| raise ValueError("give both f107 and ap, or neither")
P1916|Normal| self.jd0 = float(jd0)
P1917|Normal| self.rebuild_days = float(rebuild_days)
P1918|Normal| self.f107 = None if f107 is None else float(f107)
P1919|Normal| self.ap = None if ap is None else float(ap)
P1920|Normal| self.alts = _ALTS if alt_grid is None else np.asarray(alt_grid, dtype=float)
P1921|Normal| self.lats = _LATS if lat_grid is None else np.asarray(lat_grid, dtype=float)
P1922|Normal| self._t_tab = None      # simulated times of the tabulated fields
P1923|Normal| self._rho_tab = None    # (n_tab, n_lat, n_alt)
P1924|Normal| self._next_build = None
P1925|Normal| self._i_rho = [v.name for v in pymsis.Variable].index("MASS_DENSITY")
P1927|Normal| # -- table construction ------------------------------------------------
P1928|Normal| def _build(self, t0, t1):
P1929|Normal| """Build the local-time-averaged density tables covering simulated times [t0, t1]."""
P1930|Normal| step = self.rebuild_days * 86400.0
P1931|Normal| n = max(1, int(np.ceil((t1 - t0) / step)))
P1932|Normal| times = t0 + np.arange(n + 1) * step
P1933|Normal| if self.f107 is None:
P1934|Normal| f107s, f107as, aps = _indices(times, self.jd0)
P1935|Normal| else:
P1936|Normal| f107s = np.full(len(times), self.f107)
P1937|Normal| f107as = f107s.copy()
P1938|Normal| aps = np.full(len(times), self.ap)
P1939|Normal| rho = np.empty((len(times), len(self.lats), len(self.alts)), dtype=float)
P1940|Normal| for k in range(len(times)):
P1941|Normal| dates = _jd_to_datetime64(self.jd0 + times[k] / 86400.0)
P1942|Normal| ap7 = np.full((1, 7), float(aps[k]))
P1943|Normal| # grid mode: output shape (1, n_lon, n_lat, n_alt, n_var)
P1944|Normal| out = pymsis.calculate(dates, _LONS, self.lats, self.alts,
P1945|Normal| f107s=[f107s[k]], f107as=[f107as[k]], aps=ap7, version=2.0)
P1946|Normal| arr = np.asarray(out)[0]
P1947|Normal| rho[k] = arr[:, :, :, self._i_rho].mean(axis=0)     # mean over local time
P1948|Normal| return times, rho
P1950|Normal| def _ensure(self, t):
P1951|Normal| """Make sure tables cover simulated time t; returns the index into the table."""
P1952|Normal| if self._t_tab is None:
P1953|Normal| self._t_tab, self._rho_tab = self._build(0.0, max(t, self.rebuild_days * 86400.0))
P1954|Normal| self._next_build = self._t_tab[-1]
P1955|Normal| while t > self._next_build:
P1956|Normal| self._t_tab, self._rho_tab = self._build(self._next_build + 1.0,
P1957|Normal| self._next_build + 1.0 + self.rebuild_days * 86400.0)
P1958|Normal| self._next_build = self._t_tab[-1]
P1959|Normal| idx = int(np.clip(np.searchsorted(self._t_tab, t, side="right") - 1,
P1960|Normal| 0, len(self._t_tab) - 1))
P1961|Normal| return idx
P1963|Normal| # -- queries -----------------------------------------------------------
P1964|Normal| def density(self, alt_km, lat_deg, t_s):
P1965|Normal| """Density (kg/m^3) at geodetic altitude (km), latitude (deg), simulated time (s).
P1967|Normal| Altitudes outside the tabulated band return 0 (the atmosphere is cut off),
P1968|Normal| matching the behaviour of the exponential model above 1000 km.
P1969|Normal| """
P1970|Normal| idx = self._ensure(float(t_s))
P1971|Normal| h = float(alt_km)
P1972|Normal| la = float(lat_deg)
P1973|Normal| if h < self.alts[0] or h > self.alts[-1]:
P1974|Normal| return 0.0
P1975|Normal| field = self._rho_tab[idx]
P1976|Normal| # bilinear interpolation on the (latitude, altitude) grid
P1977|Normal| j = int(np.clip(np.searchsorted(self.alts, h) - 1, 0, len(self.alts) - 2))
P1978|Normal| k = int(np.clip(np.searchsorted(self.lats, la) - 1, 0, len(self.lats) - 2))
P1979|Normal| ha = (h - self.alts[j]) / (self.alts[j + 1] - self.alts[j])
P1980|Normal| la_ = (la - self.lats[k]) / (self.lats[k + 1] - self.lats[k])
P1981|Normal| v = (field[k, j] * (1 - ha) * (1 - la_) + field[k + 1, j] * ha * (1 - la_)
P1982|Normal| + field[k, j + 1] * (1 - ha) * la_ + field[k + 1, j + 1] * ha * la_)
P1983|Normal| return float(v)
P1985|Normal| def density_profile(self, alt_km, lat_deg, t_s):
P1986|Normal| """Density (kg/m^3) at every altitude of the tabulation grid."""
P1987|Normal| idx = self._ensure(float(t_s))
P1988|Normal| return self._rho_tab[idx, :, :].copy()

P2020|Heading 11| Abstract
P2021|Normal| Upper stages left in geostationary transfer orbit (GTO) after satellite separation can remain in orbit for decades, because their perigee is close to 250 km. This study evaluates whether such a stage can be disposed of passively with a small-satellite drag sail. The work is a numerical simulation. Model A integrates the full equations of motion with Earth oblateness (J2), atmospheric drag, lunar and solar third-body perturbations and solar radiation pressure; Model B is an orbit-averaged model with drag only. Entry geometries are sampled with a sixteen-point Latin hypercube in argument of perigee and right ascension of the ascending node, and the sail area is varied from 40 to 400 m². Lifetime is the time of first perigee passage below 120 km, found with a perigee-based event function. The atmosphere is an exponential profile, compared with NRLMSIS 2.0 through pymsis (F10.7 = 130, Ap = 10). The code is written in Python with NumPy and SciPy and uses the DOP853 integrator with relative tolerance 1e-9. Without a sail, 5 of the 16 geometries reach 120 km within 25 years, and a drag-only model overestimates the reference lifetime about eight times. With a 140 m² sail, 15 geometries comply with the exponential model and 14 with NRLMSIS. Lifetime is not a monotonic function of sail area, and the atmosphere model reverses the compliance verdict for some areas. The results should therefore be reported as a distribution over entry geometry and atmosphere model, which is relevant to domestic upper-stage design and to the 25-year disposal rule.
P2022|Normal| Keywords: passive deorbit; drag sail; geostationary transfer orbit; upper stage; orbital lifetime; NRLMSIS 2.0
